ci-verdict.yml[OQ8M]: F:Aggregates the PR verdict by polling the Checks API: fails unless every expected check on the head SHA is green, naming absent legs | R:code:.github/workflows/gov.yml, code:.github/workflows/dev-ios.yml, code:.github/workflows/dev-android.yml, code:.github/workflows/dev-harmonyos.yml, code:.github/workflows/pages.yml | A:the always-running ci-verdict check on every pull_request | S:An empty or unreadable check snapshot is a failure, never green; absence is judged only after a 120s settling window and is always named — did-not-run is never read as verified. ensure-dsh.sh[XV9L]: F:The D6 vendor pin table: fetch + sha256-verify the vendored upstream DSH runtime closure and its pinned npm dependencies | R:code:build/build.sh, code:cmake/Vendor.cmake, code:external/add-package.sh | A:the tracked provenance record; the vendored trees it materializes are UNTRACKED by design | S:Each package is vendored VERBATIM from its upstream tarball, pinned by tgz sha256; the script materializes any missing package (fresh checkout, git clean -fdX) and sha256-verifies every fetch — a re-pin changes rows here, never the trees

ensure.sh[XV8S]: F:Fetch + verify the vendored quickjs-ng engine sources; idempotent — exits 0 when the pinned tree is already on disk | R:code:cmake/Vendor.cmake, code:build/build.sh | A:run before any build that embeds the engine (desktop CLI, iOS, Android, HarmonyOS NAPI); network only on first fetch | S:The vendor tree is UNTRACKED because the engine's real-world C cannot survive the tree-sitter parse gate — the pin (commit sha + tarball sha256) living HERE is what makes disk bytes verbatim upstream

===/home/lx/dsh-mobile/src/===
CMakeLists.txt[FV8S]: F:Defines the dsh-core STATIC library: the portable C spine (rt host, wasm, socket) plus pinned quickjs-ng/wasm3/zstd TUs matching the per-host builds. | R:code:CMakeLists.txt includes this via add_subdirectory(src) only after include(Vendor) so the pins exist before the source list resolves, code:cmake/Vendor.cmake materializes those quickjs/wasm3/zstd pins into external/, code:example/CMakeLists.txt's dsh-cli links the produced dsh-core target | A:the root CMake graph is its only consumer (desktop proof build + CTest gates face); the android/harmony/iOS shells compile the same file set via their own platform builds | S:quickjs-libc excluded (rt host glues). ZSTD_DISABLE_ASM=1: subset has no .S, x86_64 link-fails on HUF_*_fast_asm_loop; no ZSTD_MULTITHREAD (D2); warnings NOT errors (D6); -include unistd.h on dsh_runtime_host.c (OHOS musl getentropy), _GNU_SOURCE stays. DSH_RELEASE=1 drives L4: host injects globalThis.__DSH_RELEASE__, JS logger drops debug/info. Platform-edge TUs (JNI/NAPI, main_cli.c, SpikeBundle.c) stay out.
build.sh[XV6T]: F:Builds the C-host desktop CLI: debug, --release, --sanitize (asan+ubsan) flavors from the shared host, quickjs-ng, vendored zstd/wasm3 | R:code:external/ensure.sh (the vendor tree), code:src/dsh_runtime_host.c (the shared host), code:example/main_cli.c (CLI main + wasmRun smoke), code:.github/workflows/c-core.yml (the sanitize flavor's CI consumer), code:build/build.sh (compile core) | A:run as sh src/build.sh [--release|--sanitize] from any cwd (self-locating); platforms never use it — Xcode/CMake-NDK/hvigor compile the same sources | S:No ZSTD_MULTITHREAD (D2 serial); -DZSTD_DISABLE_ASM=1 — the subset lacks the amd64 asm (x86_64 links undefined HUF_*). --sanitize keeps debug logging and writes BESIDE the default binary.
dsh_runtime_host.c[RG9L]: F:Shared QuickJS runtime host C shim every platform embeds: binds __dsh* intrinsics, the ESM loader over the vendored bundle, and the gateway bridge. | R:code:src/dsh_runtime_host.h declares the dsh_runtime_* lifecycle and gateway settle/event API this implements, code:runtime/dsh/upstream/shims/ node:* shims drive its proc/pty/sqlite/socket pumps, code:src/dsh_socket.c the loopback pump table behind __dshSocketPoll, code:external/ensure-dsh.sh the upstream pin DSH_UPSTREAM_VERSION mirrors | A:Compiled into the desktop CLI and the Android/Harmony host builds as the single JS-runtime core; embedders install log sink + gateway dispatch + bus sink then eval/pump and gateway settle/event drive execution. | S:Every intrinsic runs on the embedder's single serial thread; the C seam never calls JS — proc/pty/socket data reach JS only via the pump intrinsics as events (D2/D8). Unmapped specifiers and unknown settle ids fail loud. Build interface: -I<zstd> -I<zstd>/common required, platform embedders compile it WITHOUT main_cli.c. DSH_WITH_SQLITE compiles the sqlite seam out on android/harmony (shim degrades to absent-seam error); DSH_HAVE_FORKPTY absent → honest pty-unavailable error. -DDSH_RELEASE injects __DSH_RELEASE__ at bind time so the staged logger stays byte-identical to canonical.
dsh_runtime_host.h[RB8S]: F:Single-threaded embeddable C host API over quickjs-ng: new from bundle_root, eval entry module, pump microtasks, bridge gateway calls and bus lines. | R:code:runtime/dsh is the bundle_root tree dsh_runtime_new mounts (logger.js + gateway.js + manifest.json + scenario/ + vendor/), code:contract pins the v1.0.0 gateway bridge this seam dispatches (__dshGatewayCall/settle/event), code:src/dsh_runtime_host.c implements every function declared here | A:Platform embedders link it plus vendored quickjs-ng and drive new→set seams→eval→pump to completion: example/main_cli.c (macOS CLI), hosts/android/app/src/main/cpp/dsh_jni_binding.c, hosts/ios/App/Source/Bridge.h, hosts/harmony/entry/src/main/cpp/napi_init.cpp. | S:Register gateway dispatch, descriptor, and launch env BEFORE eval (later leaves JS reading "null"/"{}"); gateway_settle/gateway_event/bus_deliver must run on the eval/pump thread (hop results onto it); settling unknown call_id fails loud (-1); unsubscribed event drops (0); no extern "C" guard (tree-sitter misparses it); new() returns NULL with no error text (embedder bug); check procs_alive before teardown.
dsh_socket.c[RN8L]: F:Loopback-only TCP socket seam's whole implementation: fixed-slot server/conn tables, non-blocking pump, strict b64 write decode, per-event audit. | R:code:src/dsh_socket.h declares the ABI and loopback/audit invariants implemented here, code:src/dsh_runtime_host.c registers __dshSocketPoll and calls close_all on teardown; its serial-queue dispatch is why the tables need no lock, code:runtime/dsh/upstream/shims/node-socket-tcp.js pumps the poll every ~4ms into the node data/close event face | A:Every per-host build compiles it straight from this canonical path (src/CMakeLists.txt dsh-core, src/build.sh, the per-host build lists); dsh_runtime_host.c's gateway layer serves socketListen/socketConnect/socketWrite/socketEnd/socketClose as contract primitives, only the poll travels as the raw intrinsic | S:Loopback: listen binds INADDR_LOOPBACK only; connect accepts only the literal 127.0.0.1 — never widen here. A failed dial is terminal on its first poll and frees the slot. shutdown(SHUT_WR) defers until the write park drains. No locks: every call arrives on the runtime's serial queue (D2). Caps — 8 servers, 32 conns, 256 KiB write park; a full table refuses the accept; a closed server keeps conns addressable.
dsh_socket.h[RN8S]: F:ABI of the audited loopback TCP socket seam: listen/connect/poll/write/end/close C primitives whose whole security policy lives in this header. | R:code:src/dsh_socket.c implements this ABI; invariants and thread rules live here, code:example/main_cli.c grant-checks then serves these as socketListen/socketConnect, code:src/dsh_runtime_host.c pumps dsh_socket_poll; dsh_socket_close_all tears down, code:contract/proposals/2026-09-28-socket-seam.md is the five-rule model implemented here | A:Served to JS as the socketListen/socketConnect gateway primitives plus the per-connection data face; the JS pump polls every few ms; portable POSIX/BSD, compiled into every host's C build; a gateway that does not SERVE the seam answers unavailable (negotiation, not a branch) | S:Loopback-only fail-loud: listen binds INADDR_LOOPBACK only; connect refuses any host but the literal '127.0.0.1'. Dial is non-blocking (success = IN PROGRESS); a failed dial is terminal on the first poll and releases the slot. Audit = one structured JSON line per event, never payload bytes. Lock-free: dispatch rides the runtime's serial queue. server close shuts the listener fd only; live conns stay addressable.
dsh_wasm.c[CE7S]: F:Runs one export of an in-memory WASM module on wasm3, collecting dsh.emit output into malloc'd JSON {result,output}; NULL plus *error on failure. | R:code:src/dsh_wasm.h declares the ABI, code:external/ensure-wasm3.sh vendors the wasm3 this drives, code:hosts/ios/App/Source/Gateway/FSPrimitives.swift calls it as the iOS wasmRun primitive, code:example/main_cli.c exercises it from the CLI | A:Caller passes module bytes, an export name and an input string and frees both the returned JSON and *error; one run at a time — callers dispatch onto the runtime's serial queue, so the file-static sink needs no lock (D2) | S:Keep dsh_host_emit plain C, not m3ApiRawFunction macros: this repo's C syntax check must parse it; emit output past 64KiB is silently dropped; unlocked sink, safe only on the serial runtime queue.
dsh_wasm.h[RE8T]: F:Declares dsh_wasm_run, the contract v1.2.0 wasmRun seam: runs one export of one in-process WASM module; malloc'd JSON out, NULL plus malloc'd error. | R:code:src/dsh_wasm.c implements this header's ABI and rationale, code:contract/primitives.md specifies the wasmRun v1.2.0 primitive it realizes, code:external/ensure-wasm3.sh vendors the sha256-pinned wasm3 interpreter it embeds, code:example/main_cli.c and code:hosts/android/app/src/main/cpp/dsh_jni_scenario.c call dsh_wasm_run | A:Built into the portable C spine and exposed to platform hosts; callers pass module bytes, export name and input string, get back a malloc'd JSON string plus optional malloc'd *error, and free both; one run at a time under the gateway's serial dispatch | S:ABI fixed across hosts: input goes in the LAST 4096 bytes of module memory (offset recomputed per run; data stays below that window); all output flows through imported dsh.emit(ptr,len), i32 return is module status only; callers free both the JSON and *error. No subprocess (D2): iOS forbids, this host refuses — changing the input window or emit contract breaks every conforming WASM module.
